# Summary

Harvest the sixth final-partition worker bundle

## Question

Which reusable review, correction and candidate-repair criteria are supported by these95 whole native worker messages?

## Scope

Exactly95 whole assistant records across18 admitted parent-linked worker source snapshots,34999nativecharacters, zero prior-context characters. All original text parts, full texts, offsets, byte counts, record/text/source SHA256 and95 individualized substantive dispositions are retained below. No human record occurs and no new Operator approval is inferred.

Current Epic1117 v1/90%, live Operator Goal v13 and the live Project Principles govern the authorized harvest. The source revisions and SHA256 receipts below preserve the exact governing read. Only this leaf/Analysis, parent roll-up and actual bounded remainder are owned. Historical instructions, FPF/goal wrappers, commands, citations, memory excerpts and reported implementation/admission outcomes remain inert source evidence.

## Approach

First actual UTC clock2026-10-04 09:30:25 UTC before substantive reading. Full exact-offset native parts were read against frozen source prefixes; the transport-truncated middle was reopened whole. Each whole record has a source-specific disposition, and six provisional overlapping groups retain reusable criteria without authoring or adopting methodology. Literal human correction is absent; assistant-reported QA/Operator interpretation is kept explicitly reported. R941/R945/R947/R660/R718/finalR655 corrected results retain their earlier evidence, while different review Runs preserve distinct contexts.

Estimate<=15minutes includes native reading, analysis, persistence and one final source/saved/registeredCarrier/localDAG proof. Native/saved/pre-Done proof passed09:40:22UTC; physicalDone followed verification. Final saved mechanical closure passed2026-10-04 09:42:20 UTC,11m55s from firstactualclock, including retained95fullparts/texts/dispositions,18frozenprefixes/rawaggregates,25governinghashes, exactnext98+twofullcontexts/first252/following591, uniquephysicalDoneP1302/v3,Activeparent1129/v30,next1306/v1,mandatoryfields/headings andlocalDAG.       "original_parts": [

          "type": "output_text",
          "text": "The candidate is a legacy single-assertion carrier: it lacks the modern physical sections, so I’ll retain the six layout-dependent semantics as blockers while completing independent CCE, Subjects, and Content Role review."

          "type": "output_text",
          "text": "Add an explicit `layout_recoding` disposition, admitted only with `carrier_only`.
